-en.If enabled, Ulysses will re-open Shortcuts after attaching. = "If enabled, Ulysses will re-open Shortcuts after attaching."
-en.If enabled, Ulysses will re-open Shortcuts after creating the new sheet. = "If enabled, Ulysses will re-open Shortcuts after creating the new sheet."
-en.If enabled, the action will return a link to the image, and not its Imgur page. (WFImgurDirectLink) = "If enabled, the action will return a link to the image, and not its Imgur page."
-en.If enabled, the input images will be grouped into an album. Otherwise, the individual links will be returned. (WFImgurAlbum) = "If enabled, the input images will be grouped into an album. Otherwise, the individual links will be returned."
-en.If no file exists yet at the specified path, a new file will be created. Make sure to include a file extension (usually .txt) at the end of your path. = "If no file exists yet at the specified path, a new file will be created. Make sure to include a file extension (usually .txt) at the end of your path."
-en.If specified, only items matching all of these tags will be returned. Supports a maximum of three tags. (WFPinTags) = "If specified, only items matching all of these tags will be returned. Supports a maximum of three tags."
-en.If specified, only items matching this tag will be returned. (WFPocketItemSearchTags) = "If specified, only items matching this tag will be returned."
-en.If specified, only items with titles or URLs matching this search will be returned. (WFPocketItemSearchTerm) = "If specified, only items with titles or URLs matching this search will be returned."
-en.If the width or height is not set, that dimension is automatically calculated to maintain the original image’s aspect ratio. = "If the width or height is not set, that dimension is automatically calculated to maintain the original image’s aspect ratio."
-en.If there is already a photo album by this name, photos will be added to the existing album instead of creating a new one. (AlbumName) = "If there is already a photo album by this name, photos will be added to the existing album instead of creating a new one."

-en.If this option is enabled, and a URL is passed in, this action will fetch the title of the corresponding web page. (GetWebPageTitle) = "If this option is enabled, and a URL is passed in, this action will fetch the title of the corresponding web page."
-en.If this option is enabled, this action will get all the files inside of a folder, including its subfolders. (Recursive) = "If this option is enabled, this action will get all the files inside of a folder, including its subfolders."
-en.If you only see some but not all of your data in the results, make sure that “Allow Shortcuts to read data” is set to on in the Health app. = "If you only see some but not all of your data in the results, make sure that “Allow Shortcuts to read data” is set to on in the Health app."

-en.Show the model’s response and make additional requests before the final response is passed to the next action.
-
-**Private Cloud Compute Models**
-Use large server-based models on Private Cloud Compute to handle complex requests while protecting your privacy.
-
-**On-Device Model**
-Use the on-device model to handle simple requests without the need for a network connection.
-
-Image analysis is supported by the Private Cloud Compute and Extension models, however image generation is not supported. = "Show the model’s response and make additional requests before the final response is passed to the next action.\n\n**Private Cloud Compute Models**\nUse large server-based models on Private Cloud Compute to handle complex requests while protecting your privacy.\n\n**On-Device Model**\nUse the on-device model to handle simple requests without the need for a network connection.\n\nImage analysis is supported by the Private Cloud Compute and Extension models, however image generation is not supported."

 en.Stop and Output (Description Summary) = "Stops execution of the current shortcut, and outputs content. This action is useful when:\n• Running a shortcut from another shortcut (using the Run Shortcut action). The output will be used as the output of the Run Shortcut action.\n• Running a shortcut from Quick Actions in Finder on macOS. The output will be saved as a file alongside the files selected in Finder.\n• Running a shortcut from Services on macOS. The output will replace the selected text, if applicable.\n• Or, when running a shortcut from another location that supports output, like the command-line or the Shortcuts URL scheme.\n\nNo more actions will be run after this action."

-en.The amount of additional space between the text box and the nearest edges of the image, as a decimal proportion of the image size between 0 and 1.
